8	## What is covered
9	
10	| Area                  | File                                                                                                                                                                 | What it pins down                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
11	| --------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
12	| Worldgen determinism  | `lib/world/generation.test.ts`                                                                                                                                       | SHA-256 digests of generated worlds per seed (the save-compat contract), plus structural probes, meshing snapshots, opaque/glass layer boundaries, thin door geometry, and dungeon presence — chests/spawners/mossy generate underground and `collectDungeonSites` reproduces every chest/spawner; plus per-world-type SHA-256 baselines (flat/amplified/islands) and shape probes (flat is level, amplified higher-relief, islands floods more with dry spawn land)                           |
13	| Voxel store           | `lib/world/voxelWorld.test.ts`                                                                                                                                       | get/set/bounds/solidity and the voxel index formula saves depend on                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
14	| Raycast & collision   | `lib/world/{doors,queries}.test.ts`                                                                                                                                  | Door state/facing/bounds encoding; DDA hit/previous cells and hit distance; AABB collision edges, water non-solidity, and closed/open door panels                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
15	| Items & recipes       | `lib/game/{config,recipes,trades}.test.ts`, `lib/game/engine/systems/mining.test.ts`                                                                                 | Referential integrity: every recipe/trade/drop/armor mapping points at a real item; bow/arrow/totem/Dragon-Sword/TNT craftability; cow/pig cooking and the coal/charcoal fuel economy (coal-gated torch); `canMineBlock` tool-tier gates (coal needs a wood pickaxe); villager trades are a two-sided emerald economy of valid items; recipe-book category grouping (`recipeCategory`/`groupRecipes`) — fixed section order, craftable-now floated first                                       |
16	| Inventory algebra     | `lib/game/inventory.test.ts`                                                                                                                                         | Stack math, durable-item non-stacking, durability, crafting (including refuse-on-full), armor equip rules                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
17	| Save format           | `lib/game/save.test.ts`                                                                                                                                              | Round-trips (incl. v4→v5 migration and `lootedChests`), legacy-shape parsing and durable-stack splitting, corrupt-data rejection                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
18	| Profiles & worlds     | `lib/game/{profiles,worlds,legacyMigration}.test.ts`                                                                                                                 | Manifest CRUD and total readers (corrupt-data fallback, dangling-active repair, malformed-entry drop); seed resolution (random / numeric / text-hash); per-profile world filtering + most-recent ordering; per-world key derivation; cascade delete; the one-time legacy migration (copy-then-remove, idempotency, audio untouched)                                                                                                                                                            |
19	| Dungeon loot          | `lib/game/dungeonLoot.test.ts`                                                                                                                                       | Tiered loot tables reference real items; `seededRng` and `rollDungeonLoot` are deterministic; min/max-roll bounds                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
20	| Simulation            | `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.test.ts`                                                                                                                                 | Headless gameplay: boot, movement, hunger/regen, water, mining, doors, spear reach/throws, commands, death/respawn, night spawning, dungeon-chest lazy loot fill + the reload re-roll guard, spawner drip, the boss (summon, refusal, HP snapshot, defeat → Dragon Heart + victory), TNT torch-ignite → fuse → detonation, right-click-villager → trade station opens + a trade only crafts while open, the world-type serialize/restore round-trip and a dry Islands spawn, saves, and events |
21	| Explosions & creepers | `lib/game/engine/systems/explosion.test.ts`, `lib/game/engine/systems/mobAI.test.ts`                                                                                 | Blast clears a sphere of soft blocks (one remesh, one event), spares bedrock/spawner, falloff player/mob damage + knockback without splicing mobs; TNT prime/detonate/chain/mined-away; creeper fuse arms in range, detonates + dies, aborts on flee, and is removed (no blast) if killed first                                                                                                                                                                                                |
22	| Projectiles & ranged  | `lib/game/engine/{projectiles,systems/projectileAI,systems/combat,systems/mobAI}.test.ts`                                                                            | Arrow spawn velocity/ttl; gravity arc, swept block hit, fast-arrow anti-tunnel, mob/player hits + the `fromPlayer` filter + knockback; bow fire (ammo + durability + cooldown, refusals); skeleton kite + fire vs zombie melee                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
23	| Weather               | `lib/game/engine/systems/weather.test.ts`                                                                                                                            | Deterministic precipitation windows (seeded active/clear, mid-window intensity peak, ~rain fraction) and biome → rain/snow/clear; transient, never serialized                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
24	| Audio data            | `lib/game/audio/{materials,soundParams}.test.ts`                                                                                                                     | Every `BlockId` has a material group; every group/`MobKind` has well-formed ZZFX sound entries                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
25	| Audio schedulers      | `lib/game/audio/{footsteps,mobAmbience,musicBrain}.test.ts`                                                                                                          | Stride cadence and airborne reset; earshot gating, distance gain, look-relative pan, seeded call intervals; notes stay on scale, day/night/biome moods, deterministic under a seeded rng                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
26	| Audio routing         | `lib/game/audio/audioDirector.test.ts`                                                                                                                               | With an injected fake `SynthBackend`: events → correct sound tables, staged mining ticks, footsteps while walking, pause ducking, mute/volume application                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
27	| Audio settings        | `lib/game/audio/settings.test.ts`                                                                                                                                    | Round-trips, corrupt-data fallback, range clamping (own localStorage key, not the world save)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
28	| Skin presets          | `lib/game/{playerSkins,skinSettings}.test.ts`                                                                                                                        | Preset integrity (six unique, valid colors, default-id fallback) and preference persistence (round-trip, corrupt/unknown-id fallback, swallowed write failures)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
29	| UI sprites            | `lib/ui/*.test.ts`                                                                                                                                                   | Every item id renders a non-empty deterministic 16×16 sprite; HUD icon variants (full/half/container) differ; every skin preset renders a distinct bust portrait in its own palette                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
30	| Minimap sampling      | `lib/game/render/minimapColors.test.ts`                                                                                                                              | Top-block detection (water visible), height shading, per-block colors                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
31	| Renderer logic        | `lib/game/render/{mobVisuals,spearVisuals,projectileVisuals,heldItem,heldItemPose,extrudedSprite,itemModel,playerModel,playerPose,playerVisuals,cameraView}.test.ts` | Model lifecycle (including thrown spears and arrows), positioning, bob, item swaps, held-item poses, sprite extrusion, player-body poses/visibility, and third-person camera placement — pure Three.js, no DOM                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
32	| Particles & sky       | `lib/game/render/{particlePool,particleSystem,starField}.test.ts`                                                                                                    | Particle pool spawn/integrate/retire, drop-oldest at cap, dt clamp, determinism; hex→RGB channel split; deterministic on-sphere star positions                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

35	| Browser E2E           | `e2e/smoke.e2e.ts`                                                                                                                                                   | Real browser (booting through the profile/world menu via the seeded fixture): boot without console errors, scene draws, double-click pointer lock, input → movement, craft via UI, mining, camera view cycling, skin pick persisting across reload, pause freeze/resume, save via pause menu across reload                                                                                                                                                                                     |
36	| Browser E2E (menus)   | `e2e/menu.e2e.ts`                                                                                                                                                    | Real browser from empty storage: create a world with a fixed seed and play it; create a Superflat world and confirm level terrain near spawn; Save & Quit to Worlds then switch worlds with no page reload; reload resumes the tab's world; a second profile gets its own world list                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

```
===== components/game/ItemTooltip.test.tsx =====
useItemTooltip
shows the title and sub-lines on hover and hides on leave
renders no tooltip for null content (empty slot)
===== lib/world/lighting.test.ts =====
light classification
air and glass are fully transparent
water and leaves attenuate but transmit
solid blocks fully block light
torches and lava emit block light; ordinary blocks do not
lava blocks sky light (it is opaque) while still emitting
block light
a torch emits block light that decays one level per step
placing a torch lights a dark pocket, mining it lets the dark return
lava emits the brightest block light from an opaque source cell
skylight bake
an open world is fully lit top to bottom
sunlight falls straight down for free but stops under an opaque cap
deep inside a large solid mass is pitch dark
skylight bleeds sideways into an overhang
water attenuates skylight with depth
incremental relight (applyEdit)
the open shaft starts lit, the rock around it dark
capping the shaft plunges it into darkness
re-opening a capped shaft floods light back in
mining a side room off the shaft lights it from the shaft
a bounded edit matches a full rebake everywhere (box-rebake correctness)
a deep side edit also matches a full rebake everywhere
a long sequence of varied edits stays byte-identical to a full rebake
===== lib/game/mobLoot.test.ts =====
mob drop tables
every drop references an existing item
rng at 0 yields the minimum count of each guaranteed entry
rng near 1 yields the maximum count
a zero-minimum entry can drop nothing at the low roll
every combat mob has at least one drop entry (villagers are NPCs and drop nothing)
===== lib/game/input/inputController.test.ts =====
inputController emergency unstuck
a bare U keypress does not trigger unstuck
Shift + U triggers unstuck
inputController pointer lock
requires a double-click to start play
===== lib/game/engine/systems/breeding.test.ts =====
breeding
two fed adults nearby produce one baby and clear their love timers
far-apart fed adults do not breed
breeding stops at the passive cap
a baby grows up and regains full size
an unfed pair does not breed
new passive kinds (cow) breed through the same generic path
===== lib/game/engine/systems/randomTicks.test.ts =====
random block ticks
a sampled crop advances toward maturity
does nothing before the tick interval elapses
leaves non-crop blocks untouched
does not advance the crop when the growth roll fails
```

10	## Content expansion (cow/pig, coal, creepers/TNT, villagers/trading) — MERGED (2026-06-14), hutusi/monecraft#23 (merge commit `8079b53`), branch deleted
11	
12	User asked "do we need more mobs/items/blocks like Creepers/Villagers/Coal/Iron/Crafting Tables?" Assessment: game is content-RICH already; the gaps are mechanics+creatures, not gear tiers. Iron already exists as **Sliver Ore** (don't dup); crafting is always-on (no table gate — skip). User picked **all four** of cow+pig / coal / creeper+TNT / villagers+trading. Shipped as **one branch, one PR** (user's choice), one focused commit per phase:
13	- **`feat(mobs)` cow+pig** (`505b200`) — template clones; beef/porkchop/leather; cook recipes; breed (cow=wheat, pig=seeds). No save/worldgen change.
14	- **`feat(world)` coal** (`b58c393`) — shallow CoalOre (own PRNG `placeCoal` so existing ore/tree/structure stays byte-identical), coal+charcoal fuels, torches now cost coal+wood. **WORLDGEN_VERSION 7→8** (re-baselined the 7 byte hashes; meshing+structural probes stayed green). Schema v5.
15	- **`feat(combat)` creepers/TNT** (`94e6c44`) — reusable `explode()` primitive (`systems/explosion.ts`: batches the `blockChanges.set` chokepoint, one remesh/crater, falloff damage, only lowers mob hp so it's mid-loop-safe; tickMobs now sweeps dead AFTER the loop to catch blast kills). Creeper fuse (`MobState.fuseTimer`), TNT (torch-ignite, `state.primedTnt` map, chains). New `HOSTILE_SPAWN_MIN_RADIUS=16` (threaded via `randomLandPointNear` minRadius) so nothing spawns point-blank. No save/worldgen change (transient).
16	- **`feat(world)` villagers/trading** (`af89666`) — villager mob (passive, no-flee), **emerald** currency. KEY: a trade = `station:"villager"` recipe in `lib/game/trades.ts` spread into `RECIPES`, so it reuses the ENTIRE crafting pipeline + recipe-book UI (just extended the `station`/`craftingStation` union to add "villager"; right-click villager → `tryTradeAimedVillager` sets the station). **Village STRUCTURES deliberately deferred** — would've forced a 2nd surface-altering WORLDGEN_VERSION bump; villagers just spawn into the passive population. No save/worldgen change.

Fixed by threading the terrain config through terrainTopY/buildDungeons/placeDungeons/collectDungeonSites (engine passes worldType on load); default stayed byte-identical, the 3 new-type hashes re-rolled. CI green (verify+e2e). NOT released/tagged (still `[Unreleased]`, now holds BOTH worlds&profiles + world types). **Manual visual pass on the 3 new world types still recommended** (worldgen look not verified headless).

32	4 commits (f27c914 worldgen, 26593cc save, fa1cf1f menu UI, 2a439a7 docs). User chose: world *types* (real generation variety) over curated seeds, set = **Default/Superflat/Amplified/Islands** (terrain-config only, no biome-code changes). `generateWorld(world, worldType)` forks via `terrainConfigFor` (lib/world/generation.ts) BEFORE any block write, varying only seaLevel + per-biome baseHeight/noiseScale; caves/ores/lava/trees/structures/dungeons unchanged. **Default path byte-identical** (existing hashes untouched, NO WORLDGEN_VERSION bump); each new type pinned by its own 128³ SHA-256 baseline + structural probes. `WorldType` in lib/world/worldTypes.ts (GEN-free, so game layer imports it cleanly). Stored on `WorldMeta.worldType` + `SaveData.worldType` (engine prefers save's own, mirrors seed); schema stays v5. UI: 2×2 picker in CreateWorldForm (WORLD_TYPE_PRESETS), WorldSelect labels cards. createWorld takes worldType via the deps object (NOT a positional, to avoid breaking ~10 call sites). Islands spawn de-risked: islands keeps land gently sloped so findSpawnOnLand lands dry (engine test asserts it). Gate green (508 unit + 14 e2e). **Manual visual pass on the 3 new types still recommended** (worldgen look can't be verified headless). Awaiting user decision to push + PR.

New `WORLDGEN_VERSION` (=7) on each `WorldMeta` **replaces the whole-store `SAVE_KEY` bump** — a future worldgen change discards only stale worlds per-version (save schema stays v5). `legacyMigration.ts` folds the old single `minecraft_save_v7` + global skin into a default "Player" profile + "My World" (copy-before-delete, idempotent on profiles-manifest absence). Skin moved off `minecraft_skin_v1` onto the profile (`setProfileSkin`). All gates green (lint/typecheck/format/494 tests/build/e2e 13/13, incl. new `e2e/menu.e2e.ts`).

{
  "command": "gh pr create --repo davidhu0527/monecraft --base main --head hutusi:main \\\n  --title \"Sync fork: v0.9.0 — worlds & profiles, world types, content expansion, recipe categories\" \\\n  --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nSyncs the fork's `main` up to **v0.9.0** (31 commits, 0 behind). Four feature lines plus the release/docs housekeeping. As always, **zero new binary assets** — every texture, sprite, sound, and model is generated at runtime.\n\n## What's included\n\n**Multiple worlds & player profiles** (#21)\n- A profile (name + skin) owns a list of named worlds, like accounts on a shared console. New menu shell: profile-select → world-select → play, with create/rename/delete on both levels.\n- Two versioned localStorage manifests (`minecraft_profiles_v1`, `minecraft_worlds_v1`) above per-world save blobs; total readers (corrupt/dangling entries are repaired or dropped). A one-time migration folds an existing single save into a default \"Player\" profile + \"My World\" (copy-before-delete). Brand-new players land on the create-profile form.\n- Per-world `WORLDGEN_VERSION` replaces the old whole-store key bump: a future worldgen change discards only the affected worlds. **Save schema stays v5.**\n\n**World types** (#22)\n- Pick a generation preset when creating a world: **Default / Superflat / Amplified / Islands**. `generateWorld` forks via `terrainConfigFor` before any block write, varying only sea level + per-biome height; caves/ores/lava/trees/structures/dungeons are unchanged.\n- **Default path is byte-identical** to before (no `WORLDGEN_VERSION` bump); each new type is pinned by its own SHA-256 baseline + structural probes. Type is stored on the world meta and save blob (like seed).\n\n**Content expansion** (#23)\n- **Cows & pigs** (beef/porkchop/leather, breed + cook), **coal ore & a fuel economy** (coal/charcoal fuel; torches now cost coal + wood), **creepers + TNT + a reusable explosion primitive**, and **villagers + an emerald trading economy** (a trade is just a `station:\"villager\"` recipe reusing the whole crafting pipeline).\n- Coal bumps `WORLDGEN_VERSION` 7→8 (placed on its own PRNG so all other worldgen stays byte-identical; only the byte hashes re-rolled). Everything else here is transient/additive — no further save or worldgen change.\n\n**Recipe-book category grouping** (#24)\n- The crafting recipe book is grouped into labeled sections (Tools, Weapons, Armor, Building, Food, Materials, then station-gated Smelting and Trades), with craftable-now recipes floated to the top of each group. Categories are derived from each item's existing `kind`. Pure UI — no data/save/worldgen change.\n\n**Release & docs**\n- Cut **v0.9.0** (CHANGELOG + package.json), refreshed the README, and corrected doc drift (recipe/block counts, test-coverage map).\n\n## Compatibility notes\n- **Save schema stays at version 5** throughout; the only persistence change is the additive profile/world manifest layer (with legacy migration).\n- `WORLDGEN_VERSION` bumped once (7→8) for coal ore; world types did **not** bump it (Default is byte-identical).\n\n## Verification\n`bun run lint` · `bun run typecheck` · `bun run format:check` · `bun test` (547 pass) · `bun run build` — all green; Playwright e2e (smoke + menu flow) passing. Per-feature SHA-256 worldgen baselines and structural probes updated where the baseline legitimately changed.\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Create upstream sync PR to davidhu0527/monecraft"
}
